I'm really not sure. I can't rightly say if I'm aware or not. I don't remember noticing what everyone else is doing right in that exact moment... I just sort of realize after the fact that they've been standing there waiting on me.\n\nDoctor: Right after one of these moments ends, do you feel confused or foggy-headed?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I feel confused... or foggy. I might feel a little embarrassed that I held up the line, but my head feels just fine.\n\nDoctor: About how long do these freezing spells usually last?\n\nPatient: Well, I couldn't rightly tell you an exact time. I've never timed it... and since I don't really notice it while it's happening, it's hard to say. \n\nI guess it might just be a minute or two? I'm just not sure. It's just long enough for folks to get backed up in the line behind me, but I don't think it lasts a real long time.\n\nDoctor: Do you notice any warning signs or unusual sensations right before one of these moments happens?\n\nPatient: No, I can't say that I do. My arms and hands are steady enough. I just sort of realize after the fact that they've been standing there waiting on me.\n\nDoctor: Right after one of these moments ends, do you feel confused or foggy-headed?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I feel confused... or foggy. I might feel a little embarrassed that I held up the line, but my head feels just fine.\n\nDoctor: About how long do these freezing spells usually last?\n\nPatient: Well, I couldn't rightly tell you an exact time.
